On programs that describe more than the current request needs, LazyAgent consistently outperforms the strongest goal-stopping eager baseline by refusing unrelated work before it starts. Adding one unrelated product raises the eager bill by 22.5% and LazyAgent's by 0.0%. LazyAgent saves 42.0% of measured CPU on production scientific workflows and 51.7% of container time on a live release gate spanning four repositories. We also prove and verify exact equivalence when the request reaches the whole graph, leaving no unrelated work to avoid. Beyond permission, goal-relative output projection saves up to approximately 90% of a shared step on two third-party test suites while the identical eager control saves 0.0%; the advantage disappears when the omitted output has no other consumer or the request needs it. Ordering, reuse, and pruning can also save cost, but do not replace permission. Finally, we show that current public benchmarks are eager-shaped and contain almost no unrequested work. A pre-registered planning intervention did not broaden them. These findings motivate benchmarks built from standing programs and sequences.

</details>


### [137] [FireWorldBench: Benchmarking Complex Physical World Intelligence through Coupled-Field Fire Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23064)
*Qiang Chen,Hao Guo,Huatai Zhu,Tairan Huang,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Keke Huang,Haifeng Li,Yi Chen,Xiu Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FireWorldBench基准，通过耦合场火灾动力学评估多模态大模型与智能体的复杂物理世界智能，涵盖物理状态、时序动态、因果机制与干预推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实物理系统涉及多连续场、潜在因果机制、部分观测与干预敏感动态，仅靠物体识别、场景描述和短期视觉预测不足以理解物理世界，因此需要新基准来测评复杂物理世界智能。

Method: 构建FireWorldBench，以火灾动力学作为典型压力测试环境。基准沿两条互补轴组织：物理能力轴与火灾场景任务轴，覆盖物理状态理解、时序动态、因果机制与干预推理。包含520个火灾世界条目（494个受控仿真世界、26个真实对齐事件组），跨7个环境族、47个场景原型，结合结构化文本观测、多幅2D物理场可视化和3D事件级场景建模，生成9,074个图文交错问答对，含选择式与开放式报告生成格式。

Result: 形成包含520个条目、9,074个图文交错问答对的基准，可评估模型从多模态部分观测中推断潜在物理状态、解释机制、预测耦合场演化及评估干预后果的能力，为复杂物理世界智能提供具挑战性的测试平台。

Conclusion: FireWorldBench可作为评估多模态大模型与智能体复杂物理世界智能的挑战性测试基准，推动对潜在因果机制与干预敏感动态的理解研究。

Abstract: Understanding the physical world requires more than object recognition, scene description, and short-term visual prediction, as real-world physical systems involve multiple continuous fields, latent causal mechanisms, partial observations, and intervention-sensitive dynamics. We propose FireWorldBench, a benchmark for evaluating complex physical world intelligence in multimodal large language models and agents through coupled-field fire dynamics. Fire provides a canonical stress-test environment, where multiple interacting physical fields jointly shape observable states and temporal dynamics. FireWorldBench is organized along two complementary axes, a physical capability axis and a fire scenario task axis, jointly covering physical-state understanding, temporal dynamics, causal mechanisms, and intervention reasoning. The benchmark comprises 520 fire-world entries, including 494 controlled simulation worlds and 26 real-world-aligned event groups, spanning 47 scene archetypes across 7 environment families. These entries combine structured textual observations, multiple 2D physical-field visualizations, and 3D event-level scene modeling, yielding 9,074 text-image interleaved question-answer pairs across choice-based and open-ended report-generation formats. FireWorldBench evaluates whether models can infer latent physical states, explain underlying mechanisms, forecast coupled-field evolution, and assess intervention consequences from multimodal partial observations, providing a challenging testbed for complex physical world intelligence.

</details>


### [138] [Tutoring Large Language Models to be Domain-adaptive, Precise and Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23071)
*Somnath Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出"负责任智能"框架，从领域适应、伦理对齐、文化多语言安全三方面提升AI的安全性、伦理性和文化敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在安全、伦理与文化敏感性方面面临关键挑战，亟需一个系统性框架来构建可信、负责的下一代AI系统。

Method: 一是利用主动学习与基于图的知识改进专业领域适应，减少幻觉；二是提出一种新颖的解码时对齐机制，实时主动阻止有害文本生成，增强伦理严谨性；三是通过语言特定的引导实现文化与多语言安全，尊重多样的语言与社会规范。

Result: 在三个核心方向取得进展：降低领域幻觉、实时阻断有害生成、保障多语言与文化安全。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建兼具领域知识、伦理健全与文化适应性的下一代AI提供了蓝图。

Abstract: This thesis proposes a framework for "responsible intelligence" to address AI's critical challenges in safety, ethics, and cultural sensitivity. It advances three core areas: First, it improves domain adaptation in specialized fields using active learning and graph-based knowledge to reduce hallucinations. Second, it enhances ethical rigor via a novel decoding-time alignment mechanism that proactively blocks harmful text generation in real-time. Finally, it ensures cultural and multilingual safety through language-specific steering that respects diverse linguistic and social norms. Ultimately, this work provides a blueprint for building next-generation AI that is contextually knowledgeable, ethically sound, and culturally adaptable.

</details>


### [139] [Event Signature Transfer: Model-Agnostic Forecast Scenario Construction from Historical Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23074)
*Karthik Sridhar,Aaditya Jain,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Event Signature Transfer (EST)，一种免训练、模型无关的算子，将过去事件的"事件签名"迁移到当前预测上，把已完成事件转化为显式预测情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测者常能预判某事件即将发生，却难以把握其影响的形态、幅度与时间。需要一种无需重新训练、可迁移历史事件情景的方法。

Method: EST 先剥离源事件自身的趋势与季节性，再对剩余事件签名进行缩放和时间重设，套用到本地预测上；保持预测的关联结构，并在强度为零时精确退化为原预测。仅读取输出分位数，因此适用于任意分位数预测器，无需训练、无需模型内部信息。

Result: 在 Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5 和 Toto-2.0 上，十二个真实事件与十个合成情景中，手动配置的 EST 将真实事件样本内 WQL 降低 21.7%–90%。在 Chronos-2 上，于十一项匹配对比中胜过协变量条件化、激活编辑与原始回放。

Conclusion: EST 能够构建预测情景，但不估计该情景发生的可能性（似然）。

Abstract: Forecasters often know an event is imminent but not the shape, size, or timing of its effect. We introduce Event Signature Transfer (EST), a training-free, model-agnostic operator that turns a completed past event into an explicit forecast scenario. EST removes a source event's own trend and seasonality, then scales and retimes the remaining event signature onto a native forecast, preserving the forecast's linked structure and reducing to it exactly at zero strength. Because it reads only output quantiles, EST applies to any quantile forecaster, with no training, no model internals, at transfer time. Across twelve real episodes and ten synthetic scenarios on Chronos-2, TimesFM-2.5 and Toto-2.0, manually configured EST reduces real-episode WQL by 21.7-90\% in-sample. On Chronos-2, it leads eleven of twelve matched comparisons against covariate conditioning, activation editing and raw replay. The operator builds a scenario; it does not estimate its likelihood.

</details>


### [140] [From Inference Engine to Inference Control Plane: Connecting vLLM, llm-d, and the Evolution of Efficient Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23130)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文为综述性工作，梳理了大语言模型推理从引擎本地优化向分布式控制问题的转变，以vLLM与llm-d为互补层次，提出"推理执行规划器"及配套分类、评估框架与研究议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理正从单引擎内部优化演变为涉及可复用状态、阶段放置、异构加速器、网络、自动扩缩、可靠性与SLO的分布式控制问题，需要跨系统研究、开源实现与生产实践进行统一梳理。

Method: 综合同行评审的系统研究、开源实现与已记录的生产研究进行综述式整合；将vLLM（PagedAttention、连续批处理、内核、量化、并行）与llm-d（控制平面）视为互补层次；提出Inference Execution Planner，选择可行执行计划（聚合/分离拓扑、KV来源与传输动作、硬件选型、路由/准入策略、扩缩决策）；并构建来源本地化的基准图集、瓶颈迁移分类法、部署指南与基于SLO-goodput的评估框架。

Result: 本文贡献为综合性梳理而非新基准，所有性能与部署结果均归因于原始来源；证据表明现代推理的稀缺资源正从单纯原始FLOPs转向受管状态、放置、网络移动、可靠性与决策质量。具体指标结果请参见各原始文献。

Conclusion: 现代LLM推理的核心瓶颈已从算力转向状态管理、放置与网络等系统要素，需要从"仅选端点"升级为"选执行计划"的规划式控制；作者据此给出基准图集、瓶颈迁移分类、部署指南、SLO-goodput评估框架，并提出面向智能体、多模态、异构与韧性推理的研究问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is evolving from an engine-local optimization problem into a distributed control problem involving reusable state, phase placement, heterogeneous accelerators, networking, autoscaling, reliability, and service-level objectives. This paper connects that transition across peer-reviewed systems research, open-source implementations, and documented production studies. It treats vLLM and llm-d as complementary layers: model-serving engines optimize execution through mechanisms such as PagedAttention, continuous batching, kernels, quantization, and parallelism, while an inference control plane can optimize where, when, and under what policy execution occurs across a fleet. The contribution is synthesis rather than a new benchmark; all reported performance and deployment results remain attributed to their original sources. The combined evidence suggests that the scarce resource in modern inference is shifting from raw FLOPs alone toward managed state, placement, network movement, reliability, and decision quality. We propose an Inference Execution Planner that selects feasible execution plans rather than only endpoints, including aggregated versus disaggregated topology, KV source and transfer action, hardware variant, routing/admission policy, and slower scaling decisions. We also provide a source-local benchmark atlas, a bottleneck-migration taxonomy, practical deployment guidance, an evaluation framework based on SLO-goodput, and research questions for agentic, multimodal, heterogeneous, and resilient inference.

</details>


### [141] [CraftBench-UE: Deterministic Evaluation for Coding Agents in Unreal Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23142)
*Shutong Wu,Kevin Calderone,Andy Tsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CraftBenchUE 评测框架，在隔离的 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体并重建其提交，用确定性构建、资产与运行时检查（无需 LLM 裁判）评估游戏玩法实现能力，包含 70 个任务并对比 C++ 与 Blueprint 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在游戏引擎中实现玩法特性不只是写代码：代码能编译运行并不意味着实现了所要求的玩法。现有评测缺乏针对引擎环境下真实玩法实现的可靠评估手段，因此需要一套不依赖 LLM 裁判、可确定性验证的评测框架。

Method: 构建 CraftBenchUE 评测框架：在隔离的 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体，在新的工程中重建其保存的提交，并施加确定性的构建、资产与运行时检查，不使用 LLM 裁判。基于该框架搭建含 70 个任务的基准，覆盖 C++ 源码、蓝图资产与编辑器脚本；在两种编辑器工具配置下评测 7 个模型，并在 C++ 任务上设置文件与 shell 基线。此外设计 10 组配对任务：玩法规格与运行时测试相同，但交付物分别要求 C++ 与 Blueprint。

Result: 在 10 组配对任务中，C++ 完成率在两个工具配置下分别比 Blueprint 高出 30.0 和 42.9 个百分点。在配对集中按时提交且通过资产检查的 Blueprint 提交里，分别有 42.2% 和 50.0% 未通过显式运行时断言——它们满足资产要求却未通过所要求的玩法测试。

Conclusion: 代码可编译运行与真正实现玩法存在显著差距，Blueprint 任务尤其容易出现"资产合规但玩法失败"的情况。作者将随报告发布该评测框架、任务基准及轨迹发现。

Abstract: Building gameplay features in a game engine requires more than code, as code that compiles and runs does not necessarily implement the requested gameplay. We introduce CraftBenchUE, an evaluation harness that runs agents in an isolated Unreal Engine environment, reconstructs their saved submissions in fresh projects, and applies deterministic build, asset, and runtime checks without an LLM judge. Based on the harness, we built a benchmark consisting of 70 tasks spanning C++ source, Blueprint assets, and editor scripting. We evaluate seven models under two editor-tool configurations, with a file-and-shell baseline on C++ tasks. We further pair tasks that specify the same gameplay and use the same runtime tests, but require C++ and Blueprint as the deliverables. Across the 10 paired tasks, C++ completion rates exceed Blueprint by 30.0 and 42.9 percentage points in the two tool configurations. Among on-time Blueprint submissions in this paired set that pass asset checks, 42.2% and 50.0% fail explicit runtime assertions. These submissions satisfy asset requirements but fail the required gameplay tests. We will release the harness, task benchmark, and our trajectory findings with the report.

</details>


### [142] [Do Not Trust the Benchmark: Limitations of General LLM Rankings and a Case for Task-Specific Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23201)
*Danial Amin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出通用大模型排行榜存在五类相互关联的局限，主张评估应披露配置、验证题目、并报告成本与时间，同时介绍众包平台 Isotanta 作为实践示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数日益影响大模型的开发、营销与选型，但总分只有在明确被测系统、题目内容和评估条件时才具有可解释性，而当前通用排行榜往往缺乏这些关键信息。

Method: 采用观点性（perspective）分析与案例论证，梳理五类关联局限：被测系统与公开系统的差异、外部评估中的商业动机与依赖、基准饱和与题目缺陷及数据污染、模型针对评分程序的取巧行为、通用分数与用户实际任务的相关性不足；并以众包基准平台 Isotanta 为例，讨论贡献式题目与重复评估。

Result: 论证了各类问题需要不同的应对方式；扩大题目池可提升任务覆盖度，重复采样可提升估计稳定性，但二者均不能保证评估效度或个性化。论文还区分了平台现有的共享排名与所提出的任务特定、用户自供评估。

Conclusion: 模型选型需要的是关于目标任务表现的证据，而非仅仅在通用排行榜上的高名次；评估程序应披露测试配置、验证题目与任务完成情况、报告性能与成本及执行时间，并明确泛化范围。

Abstract: Benchmark scores increasingly influence the development, marketing, and selection of large language models (LLMs). Yet an overall score is interpretable only in relation to the system tested, the questions included, and the conditions of evaluation. This perspective examines five connected limitations of general LLM rankings: differences between evaluated and publicly available systems; commercial incentives and dependencies in external evaluation; benchmark saturation, defective tests, and data contamination; models exploiting scoring procedures; and the limited relevance of general scores to users' tasks. Documented cases illustrate why these problems require different responses. I argue for evaluation procedures that disclose the tested configuration, validate questions and successful task completion, report performance alongside cost and execution time, and make the scope of generalization explicit. I then discuss \textbf{Isotanta}, a crowdsourced benchmarking platform, as a practical example of contributed questions and repeated evaluation. A larger question pool may improve task coverage, while repeated sampling can improve the stability of estimates on that pool; neither guarantees validity or personalization. The paper distinguishes the platform's current shared ranking from proposed task-specific and user-provided evaluations. Its central argument is that model selection requires evidence about performance on the intended work, not simply a high position on a general leaderboard.

</details>


### [143] [Expansion Counts under Standard A* Tie-Breaking Strategies on the Final Plateau](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23293)
*Alex Fukunaga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了A*搜索中相同f值节点的平局打破策略，证明在一致启发式下九种标准策略之间可存在任意大的扩展差距，并分析了启发式权重对平局敏感性的消除作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A*算法中，相同f值节点的平局打破策略决定了最终f层上A*扩展哪些状态，但这一选择对搜索性能的系统性影响尚不清晰，缺乏对策略间优劣的理论刻画。

Method: 针对九种标准平局打破策略，在一致启发式假设下构造正代价实例进行对比分析；使用参数化的单位代价网格例子研究low-h配合FIFO与LIFO的扩展数比值；并在非目标点h>0的单位代价搜索中，分析完美区域内精确启发式值的极值行为，以及评估函数f_α=g+αh（0≤α<1）下的平局消除性质。

Result: 证明任意两种策略之间都存在正代价实例，使一方相对另一方获得任意大的加性扩展优势；单位代价网格例子给出low-h配合FIFO与LIFO之间无界的扩展数比值；在h精确时得到互补极值结果：low-h最小化完美区域内剩余扩展数，high-h最大化总扩展数；当0≤α<1且非目标点h>0时，每个启发式权重都消除平局敏感性。

Conclusion: 平局打破策略在一致启发式下本质上不可比较，策略选择会对扩展数产生任意大的影响；而引入小于1的启发式权重α可完全消除平局敏感性，使所有平局策略扩展相同状态集，为A*的稳健实现提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In the A* search algorithm, the tie-breaking strategies for nodes with the same $f$-value determines which states A* expands on the final $f$-layer. For nine standard tie-breaking strategies, we show that under a consistent heuristic, every pair has positive-cost instances favoring each strategy over the other by an arbitrarily large additive expansion gap. A parameterized unit-cost grid example also gives unbounded expansion-count ratios between low-$h$ with FIFO and LIFO. In unit-cost search with $h > 0$ at non-goals, exact heuristic values near the goal lead to complementary extremal results: low-$h$ minimizes the number of remaining expansions from a common configuration within the perfect region, while high-$h$ maximizes the total number of expansions when every final-plateau state with $h=1$ is a goal predecessor. Finally, with the evaluation function $f_α = g + αh$, when $h>0$ at non-goals, every heuristic weight $0 \leq α<1$ eliminates tie-breaking sensitivity, and all tie-breaking strategies expand the same set of states.

</details>


### [144] [TicTacBench: Benchmarking Timing Closure Capabilities of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23363)
*Bowei Wang,Zhigang Fang,Zhijie Yang,Renzhi Chen,Shanshan Li,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TicTacBench基准，评估编码智能体在post-PnR场景下的RTL时序收敛能力，并给出改进方法TicTacSkill。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL基准主要评估功能正确性与PPA，忽视了对智能体时序收敛（timing closure）能力的评估。

Method: TicTacBench包含30个多样化任务，每个任务提供次优RTL设计、真实时序约束、功能等价性验证与时序报告，在post-PnR下评估；使用8个前沿LLM驱动智能体进行300多次运行；并提出TicTacSkill，引导智能体遵循标准时序收敛流程。

Result: 最佳智能体仅能收敛53.3%的任务，平均ADP退化7.18%，EDDP改善8.83%；归纳出常见失败类别；TicTacSkill将时序收敛率提升9%。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在RTL设计上进步显著，但时序收敛能力仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led to the emergence of coding agents capable of performing complex engineering tasks, including register-transfer level (RTL) design and optimization. Existing RTL benchmarks mainly evaluate functional correctness and performance, power, and area (PPA) of the generated RTL designs, leaving agents' ability for \emph{timing closure} under-evaluated. We propose TicTacBench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate coding agents' capabilities for RTL-level timing closure under post-place-and-route (post-PnR) evaluation. TicTacBench contains 30 diverse tasks, each provided with a suboptimal RTL design, realistic timing constraints, functional equivalence verification, and timing reports. With over 300 runs of coding agents driven by 8 frontier LLMs, we find that even the best agent can only close 53.3\% of tasks with 7.18\% area-delay product (ADP) degradation and 8.83\% energy-delay-squared product (EDDP) improvement on average. We identify common failure categories that explain why agents fail to close timing. Then we propose TicTacSkill, a new method that guides agents to follow standard timing-closure procedures and improves the Timing Closure Rate by 9\%. These results suggest that while coding agents have made significant progress in RTL design, their timing-closure capability still has substantial room for improvement.

</details>


### [145] [Leaky-integrator reconstruction: taming error accumulation in recursive differenced time-series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23378)
*Zijiang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"漏积分器重建"（leaky-integrator reconstruction），一种免训练方法，用于修复递归差分预测中的误差累积问题，通过将积分器极点移入单位圆内来限制误差方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归差分预测（预测一步差分再累积求和）是处理非平稳性的标准做法，但作者指出这本质上是一个极点在单位圆上的离散积分器，会导致非线性模型递归展开时发散，误差随时间急剧放大。因此需要一种无需重新训练即可修复该病态的方法。

Method: 提出漏积分器重建，将积分器传递函数由 H(z)=1/(1-z^-1) 改为 H(z)=1/(1-γz^-1)（γ<1），即把极点移到单位圆内，理论上可限制累积误差方差。该方法在重建阶段应用，采用单一固定参数 γ=0.9，无需重新训练，对任何已部署的一步预测器或基础模型仅需两行代码改动。

Result: 在七种发散架构和二十个数据集上，误差在每个预测时域都缩小，平均增益从 H=24 时约3%增长到 H=96 时23%、H=192 时37%、H=336 时51%（各架构43-74%）；使用最优极点时可达78%。诊断实验显示，336步误差达到良好预测器的数倍（归一化MAE 1.6-3.8 对约0.8）。

Conclusion: 漏积分器重建可证明地在不存在病态的情形下保持惰性（稳定或联合预测器已达不可约误差率），因此是一种安全、通用的默认选择。

Abstract: We introduce leaky-integrator reconstruction, a training-free method that cures the error accumulation of recursive differenced forecasting. Our first contribution is diagnostic: predicting one-step changes and integrating them by cumulative summation, the standard remedy for non-stationarity, is a discrete integrator with a pole on the unit circle, and we show this makes recursive rollout of a nonlinear model diverge, its 336-step error reaching several times that of a well-behaved forecaster (normalised MAE 1.6-3.8 versus about 0.8) across every neural architecture tested. Our second, central contribution is the fix: move the pole inside the unit circle with a leaky integrator H(z) = 1/(1 - gamma z^-1), gamma < 1, which provably bounds the accumulated error variance. Applied at reconstruction time with a single fixed gamma=0.9 (no retraining, a two-line change to any deployed one-step or foundation-model forecaster), it shrinks error at every horizon, the mean gain over seven diverging architectures and twenty datasets growing from ~3% at H=24 to 23% at H=96, 37% at H=192 and 51% (43-74% across those architectures) at H=336 (78% with an oracle pole). Crucially, it is provably inert where no pathology exists (stable or joint predictors already at the irreducible rate), making it a safe, general default.

</details>


### [146] [AgentBetta: Verification-Driven Adaptive Configuration of an AI Nano-Agent through Selective Expansion and Verified Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23512)
*Md. Ashraful Babu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了自适应 AI 微型智能体框架 AgentBetta，将模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆和算力等因素视为可执行配置，并通过验证驱动的诊断、选择性扩展和反事实收缩进行动态更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体通常以预定义配置部署，但不同任务所需的模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆和计算资源差异显著，固定配置可能导致能力不足或资源浪费。

Method: 将配置因素表示为可执行配置，通过验证驱动的诊断识别配置缺陷，选择性扩展相关维度，并基于验证进行反事实收缩；在 AB-ConfigBench 上进行受控机制验证，并与外部智能体比较。

Result: 在 AB-ConfigBench 上实现 91.38% 的验证成功率，同时将中位上下文字符从 64,000 降至 8,000，将中位工具暴露从五个降至零；配置缺陷诊断宏 F1 为 0.819、精度为 1.000；成功后的收缩在 56.41% 的单维度收缩探测中保持验证结果。

Conclusion: 外部评估显示自适应配置可改善验证任务完成与能力暴露之间的平衡，但结果因基准和智能体家族而异；跨家族复制未重现主骨干的准确率排序，专用系统在某些任务领域仍有优势。因此 AgentBetta 应被理解为调节能力分配和推理开销的配置适应机制，而非专用智能体架构的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents are typically deployed with predefined configurations, although the required model capability, context, tools, permissions, memory, and computational resources can vary substantially across tasks. This study develops and evaluates AgentBetta, an adaptive AI Nano-Agent framework that represents these factors as an executable configuration and updates them through verification-driven diagnosis, selective expansion, and verification-based counterfactual contraction. The evaluation distinguishes controlled mechanism validation from external agent comparisons. On the AB-ConfigBench benchmark, AgentBetta achieved 91.38% verified success while reducing median context allocation from 64,000 to 8,000 context characters and median tool exposure from five tools to zero compared with the fully provisioned configuration. The configuration-deficiency diagnosis achieved a macro-F1 score of 0.819 with precision of 1.000 across the evaluated dimensions, and selective expansion avoided unnecessary changes to unrelated configuration dimensions. Post-success contraction preserved verification outcomes in 56.41% of evaluated one-dimension contraction probes, indicating that some successful configurations contained removable capability under the tested conditions. External evaluations indicate that adaptive configuration can improve the balance between verified task completion and capability exposure; however, the results vary across benchmarks and agent families. In particular, the cross-family replication did not reproduce the primary-backbone accuracy ordering, and specialized systems remained advantageous for certain task domains. These results support interpreting AgentBetta as a configuration-adaptation mechanism that regulates capability allocation and inference expenditure rather than as a universal replacement for specialized agent architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [CHOREO: Every Humanoid Skill as a Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22274)
*Ziyi Sun,Jingwen Chen,Yuxi Wang,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yujun Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CHOREO框架，将异构人形机器人技能统一表示为可执行运动轨迹（SkillMotion），无需重新训练或测试时更新模型即可组合技能，在Unitree G1上实现95.4%的多动作任务序列成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人技能虽经强化学习、动作模仿和生成建模等方式获得多样能力，但因表示、接口和控制器不兼容而彼此孤立，难以组合复用。

Method: 核心观察是任何技能最终都可表达为可执行运动轨迹。CHOREO将每种能力转换为SkillMotion统一表示，融合运动状态、接触、语义和边界条件，并通过直接衔接、三次Hermite混合或经校验的桥接动作来组合技能，无需重训源模型或测试时更新。

Result: 在MuJoCo的Unitree G1平台上，CHOREO组织了来自异构源的2,950个SkillMotion资产，在130个多动作任务中达到95.4%序列成功率，八动作序列成功率为93.8%。

Conclusion: 可执行轨迹为积累和组合预训练人形技能提供了可扩展的接口，验证了训练无关的异构技能组合的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics have produced diverse skills through reinforcement learning, motion imitation, and generative modeling. Yet these capabilities remain siloed because they are built around incompatible representations, interfaces, and controllers. We present CHOREO, a framework for training-free composition of heterogeneous humanoid skills. Our key observation is that, regardless of how a skill is learned, it can ultimately be expressed as an executable motion trajectory. Based on this observation, CHOREO converts each capability into SkillMotion, a unified representation that combines motion states, contacts, semantics, and boundary conditions. Skills are composed through direct continuation, cubic Hermite blending, or validated bridge motions, without retraining source models or updating models at test time. On Unitree G1 in MuJoCo, CHOREO organizes 2,950 admitted SkillMotion assets derived from heterogeneous sources and achieves 95.4\% sequence success across 130 multi-action tasks, including 93.8\% success on eight-action sequences. These results demonstrate that executable trajectories provide a scalable interface for accumulating and composing pretrained humanoid capabilities.

</details>


### [148] [D3DWA: Adaptive Weight and Prediction-Horizon for Dynamic Window Approach via Dueling Double Deep Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22276)
*Zahra Jooyandeh,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 D3DWA，用 Dueling Double DQN 在每个控制步自适应联合选择 DWA 的评价权重与预测时域，保留 DWA 的轨迹生成与碰撞检测。八类仿真环境（含未见布局）全部到达目标；实机三组配置全部完成，其中仅调权重的变体在受限场景超时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWA 广泛用于局部导航，但性能高度依赖导航前固定的参数。尤其是合适的预测时域应随局部自由空间变化：开阔区域宜用较长时域以实现高效运动，狭窄或杂乱区域宜用较短时域以保持可行运动。因此需要一种能在线自适应调整的方法。

Method: 提出 D3DWA 自适应 DWA 框架，基于 Dueling Double Deep Q-Network（D3QN）。在每个控制步，从连续导航状态中联合选择 DWA 的评价权重与预测时域，同时保留 DWA 原有的轨迹生成与碰撞检测机制。

Result: 在八个仿真环境中（包括未见过的布局），D3DWA 均到达所有目标。实机实验表明 D3DWA 完成了全部三组测试配置，其中包括一个受限场景——仅自适应权重的变体在该场景中超时失败。

Conclusion: 结果证明了联合自适应评价权重与预测时域的优势，相比仅调整权重的方式更能应对受限与受限空间。补充材料见 https://mertcookimg.github.io/d3dwa/

Abstract: The Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) is widely used for local navigation, but its performance depends strongly on parameters that are typically fixed before navigation. In particular, the appropriate prediction horizon can vary with local free space: longer horizons support efficient motion in open areas, whereas shorter horizons help preserve feasible motions in narrow or cluttered regions. This paper proposes D3DWA, an adaptive DWA framework based on a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network (D3QN), which jointly selects the DWA evaluation weights and prediction horizon from a continuous navigation state at every control step while retaining DWA's trajectory generation and collision checking. In eight simulated environments, including unseen layouts, D3DWA reached every goal. Real-robot experiments further showed that D3DWA completed all three tested configurations, including a constrained case in which the weights-only variant timed out. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly adapting the evaluation weights and prediction horizon. Additional material is available at https://mertcookimg.github.io/d3dwa/

</details>


### [149] [DeViGrasp: Robust Visual Mobile Grasping for Quadruped Manipulators under Degraded Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22278)
*Liang Zhou,Jiaming Su,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向视觉退化场景的移动抓取基准DeViGrasp-Bench与师生框架DeViGrasp-Net，通过状态条件抓取推理和可靠性感知时序目标估计提升四足机械臂全身体控制的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂虽能实现复杂环境中的移动抓取，但其全身体控制策略对机载视觉感知的不可靠性十分敏感。现有方法多在较可靠观测条件下开发，未系统考察遮挡、分割掩码丢失、深度噪声与目标定位抖动对抓取推理和目标跟踪的影响，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建DeViGrasp-Bench基准，引入可控视觉退化、可见与未见过物体、多难度等级和复杂地形，并评估任务成功率、执行效率与动作平滑度。提出DeViGrasp-Net师生框架：特权教师基于物体、机器人、末端执行器和任务状态对离线抓取候选进行注意力选择；可部署学生通过Target Hold Memory与Temporal Memory Attention融合双视角分割深度观测及当前、记忆、恢复目标假设。

Result: DeViGrasp-Net在各退化等级、未见过物体和复杂地形上均优于VBC，并在所有评估退化等级上超越适配后的DQ-Net。Difficult设置下成功率62.3%，较VBC和DQ-Net分别提升16.1与4.3个百分点；Hard设置下对DQ-Net的优势扩大至10.5个百分点。消融实验证实抓取感知监督与可靠性感知时序记忆的互补增益。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了退化视觉下移动抓取评测与鲁棒控制的空白，验证了状态条件抓取推理与可靠性感知时序记忆相结合的有效性，为四足机械臂在不可靠感知下的移动抓取提供了基准与方法支撑。

Abstract: Quadruped manipulators enable mobile grasping in complex environments, yet their whole-body control policies remain vulnerable to unreliable onboard visual perception. Existing methods are typically developed under relatively reliable observations and have not systematically examined how occlusion, segmentation-mask dropout, depth noise, and target-localization jitter affect grasp reasoning and target tracking. To address this gap, we introduce DeViGrasp-Bench, a benchmark for mobile grasping under degraded vision that incorporates controlled visual degradations, seen and unseen objects, multiple difficulty levels, and complex terrains, and evaluates task success, execution efficiency, and action smoothness. We further propose DeViGrasp-Net, a teacher--student framework that combines state-conditioned grasp reasoning with reliability-aware temporal target estimation. The privileged teacher attends to offline grasp candidates conditioned on object, robot, end-effector, and task states, while the deployable student fuses dual-view segmented-depth observations with current, memory, and recovery target hypotheses through Target Hold Memory and Temporal Memory Attention. DeViGrasp-Net outperforms VBC across degradation levels, unseen objects, and complex terrains, and surpasses an adapted DQ-Net across all evaluated degradation levels. Under the Difficult setting, it achieves a success rate of 62.3\%, improving upon VBC and DQ-Net by 16.1 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively; under the Hard setting, its margin over DQ-Net increases to 10.5 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of grasp-aware supervision and reliability-aware temporal memory.

</details>


### [150] [ORDER: A Fictitious-World Benchmark for Domain-Adaptive Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22285)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Anuj Tiwari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ORDER基准（虚构物理世界语料+知识测试ORDER-BENCH+组合排序任务ORDER-SPATIAL），用于干净评估持续预训练中的真实学习而非记忆，并验证离线小模型在机器人规划上可超越GPT-4.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练领域评估存在根本问题：若训练语料与模型已有知识重叠，性能提升无法区分是"新学习"还是"已有知识"。该问题在知识密集、任务简单（KHTL）的机器人场景（如药品分发、危化品处理、设施专属规程）尤为关键——规则专有且安全攸关，实地测试代价高甚至不安全。

Method: 构建ORDER基准，基于虚构世界：342,069 token的合成语料定义了一个自洽且不可能出现在任何预训练数据中的物理体系。包含500题知识测试（ORDER-BENCH）与更难组合任务ORDER-SPATIAL（在熟悉与全新场景中为安全操作排序物体）。随后接入机器人管线：持续预训练→技能适配阶段，并在仿真iiwa7机械臂上打通"感知到执行"全流程，含人类在环纠正。

Result: 未适配的GPT-4.1在ORDER-SPATIAL上低于随机水平（Kendall's tau=0.441），说明其先验与虚构物理冲突；持续预训练后小模型在熟悉和全新场景均显著提升，表明是真实世界模型归纳而非记忆。仅通过知识测试的模型往往无法产出可执行计划；经技能适配后，全离线小模型在GPT-4.1可检索同样规则的情况下仍胜出（tau=0.848 vs 0.606）。

Conclusion: ORDER-SPATIAL性能（而非知识测试准确率）才真正预测实际规划质量；知识问答能力不等于可执行规划能力，技能适配阶段不可或缺。基准为持续预训练提供无污染评估路径。

Abstract: Adapting language models to new domains via continual pre-training raises a basic evaluation problem: if the training corpus overlaps with what the model already knows, performance gains cannot be cleanly attributed to new learning rather than pre-existing knowledge. This matters most for knowledge-intensive, task-light (KHTL) robot deployments - pharmaceutical dispensing, hazardous-material handling, facility-specific protocols, where the physical task is simple but the governing rules are proprietary and safety-critical, and where extensive live testing is costly or unsafe. We introduce ORDER (Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning), a benchmark built on a fictitious world: a 342,069-token synthetic corpus defining a self-consistent physics that cannot appear in any model's pre-training data. ORDER pairs a 500-question knowledge test (ORDER-BENCH) with a harder compositional task, ORDER-SPATIAL: ordering objects for safe manipulation across both familiar and entirely novel scenes. GPT-4.1 without adaptation scores below chance on ORDER-SPATIAL (Kendall's tau = 0.441), showing its priors actively conflict with the invented physics. After continual pre-training, small models improve substantially on both familiar and novel scenes alike evidence of genuine world-model induction rather than memorization. We then carry this through to a robot pipeline: models that answer the knowledge test well often cannot produce valid, executable plans without a further skill-adaptation stage, after which small, fully offline models outperform GPT-4.1 even when GPT-4.1 is given retrieval access to the same rules (Kendall's tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606), on a full perception-to-execution loop demonstrated on a simulated iiwa7 arm with human-in-the-loop correction. Throughout, ORDER-SPATIAL performance, not knowledge-test accuracy is what predicts real plan quality.

</details>


### [151] [OJOx: Specification-Conditioned Demonstrations for Embodied AI in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22289)
*Mohamed Dawod*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"规格条件演示"（specification-conditioned demonstration）这一新数据范式，并构建了面向建筑场景的采集接口 OJOx，可同步记录设计规格、物理感知画面与全身/手部动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心与全身人类演示数据能记录人"看到什么、做了什么"，却几乎不记录赋予动作目的的外部规格。在建筑等技能性工作中，任务目标由设计模型定义的项目专属构型决定，且该构型在演示现场往往尚不存在；工人真正可迁移的能力是从规格实现新几何，而非复制某堵墙的形状。缺失规格信息会使模型只能模仿动作，无法学会面向训练中未见规格行动。

Method: 提出规格条件演示：将被演示者感知的物理状态、由外部设计提供的意图状态、以及连接二者的行为进行同步记录。实现采集接口 OJOx：把设计几何送入头显并锚定在物理工作空间中，渲染进演示者的双目透视视图，同时同步录制该视图与全身及手部运动。开展一次完整装备的搭建会话——一面33个构件的墙体，其规格在工作推进过程中动态变化——并用独立配准的外部相机对记录与物理场景进行核验。记录结果兼容现有人形机器人重定向基础设施，并可在仿真中回放到 Unitree G1。

Result: 完成一次全流程仪器化会话：砌筑33构件墙，规格在工作中途变更；通过独立配准的外部相机验证记录与物理场景一致；录制数据可兼容人形机器人重定向管线并在仿真中回放至 Unitree G1。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种数据接口，用于检验具身策略能否不仅模仿已演示动作，还能朝训练经验中未曾出现的规格去行动，从而支撑可迁移技能学习的研究。

Abstract: Large-scale egocentric and whole-body human demonstrations are becoming a primary source of data for embodied intelligence. They record what people perceive and do, but rarely the external specification that gave an action its purpose. In construction that omission is consequential: skilled work is directed at project-specific configurations defined in a design model - configurations not yet present in the environment being observed. A mason's transferable competence is not the geometry of one wall but the ability to realise a new geometry from a specification. We introduce the specification-conditioned demonstration: a synchronised record of the physical state a demonstrator perceives, the intended state supplied to them by an external design, and the behaviour connecting the two. We present OJOx, a capture interface that realises this for construction - delivering design geometry to a headset, anchoring it in the physical workspace, rendering it into a demonstrator's stereo passthrough view, and recording that view synchronously with whole-body and hand motion. We report one fully instrumented session - a 33-component wall laid against a specification that changes while the work proceeds - and check the record against the physical scene through an external camera registered independently of the capture. Recorded sessions remain compatible with existing humanoid retargeting infrastructure and replay onto a Unitree G1 in simulation. The result is a data interface for testing whether embodied policies can learn not merely to imitate demonstrated actions, but to act toward specifications absent from their training experience.

</details>


### [152] [When Does Test-Time Physical Diagnosis Pay? A Frozen Policy Buys Evidence It Never Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22299)
*Zhengshu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用六个有序经验条件组成的"诊断链"来评估机器人对不熟悉物理条件的自适应能力，并发现链条常在"证据使用"环节断裂，聚合准确率会掩盖这一失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人面对陌生物理条件时通常会采集证据并自适应，但"诊断能改善行为"并非自动成立。作者希望明确这种自适应应满足的完整经验条件，并检验现有方法是否真正完成了从诊断到有用行为的全链条，而非仅凭恢复准确率或聚合性能下结论。

Method: 作者提出六个有序经验条件：有意义的参照、物理条件的可识别性、所获证据的使用、决策价值、相对固定备选方案的选择价值、以及安全实现。在受控环境与公开环境中检验这一链条，并迁移到未见过的机制上测试；同时用仅依赖轨迹增量的线性模型作为对照，以判断轨迹信息是否具有信息量却未被使用。

Result: 在受控环境中，链条端到端成立。但迁移到未见机制后，链条在"证据使用"处断裂：在需要完整轨迹的决策上，冻结的解码器不改变选择；而仅用轨迹增量的线性模型在未参与拟合的机制上却能恢复正确选择，说明轨迹本身有信息但未被使用。失效集中在信息最丰富的证据层级——这些决策降至随机水平，而用低成本证据即可解决的决策仍保持正确，这种分化被聚合准确率掩盖。在公开环境中，链条也可能在其他环节断裂。

Conclusion: 成功识别物理条件既不能保证证据被使用，也不能保证有用的自适应。评估不应仅依赖恢复准确率或聚合性能，而应定位诊断链具体在哪个环节断裂。

Abstract: When a robot faces unfamiliar physical conditions, a common approach is to collect evidence about what changed and adapt. For such diagnosis to improve behavior, six ordered empirical conditions must hold: a meaningful reference, identifiability of the physical condition, use of the acquired evidence, decision value, selection value over a fixed alternative, and safe realization. We test this chain in controlled and public environments. It holds end to end in our controlled environments. After transfer to unseen mechanisms, however, it breaks at evidence use. On decisions requiring the full trace, the frozen decoder does not change its choice. A linear model using only trace increments recovers the correct choice on mechanisms excluded from fitting, showing that the trace is informative but unused. The failure is concentrated at the richest evidence level: those decisions fall to chance, while decisions settled with lower-cost evidence remain correct, a split hidden by aggregate accuracy. The same chain can fail at other links in public environments. Successful physical identification therefore guarantees neither evidence use nor useful adaptation; evaluation should identify where the chain breaks rather than rely on recovery accuracy or aggregate performance alone.

</details>


### [153] [EditWM: Event-Decomposed World Modeling with Incremental Correction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22317)
*Junjie Yang,Qingwei Zeng,Youyou Li,Zicheng Ding,Ziyi Shi,Shuqi Shen,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EditWM 世界模型，将未来预测分解为正常演化与事件驱动的增量校正，在紧凑视觉特征空间中实现，并用于轨迹评分，在 NAVSIM 上取得未来特征 MSE 降低 5.35% 与 91.05 EPDMS 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶世界模型中，驾驶动态的可预测性存在差异：常规演化与事件引发的偏差需要区别对待，后者需要选择性校正，而统一预测难以兼顾。

Method: EditWM 在紧凑视觉特征空间中将未来预测分解为轨迹条件化的正常预测器（提供基础预测并冻结）与校正解码器。校正解码器对比基础预测、观测历史与规划动作，生成有界特征更新，并由学习门控调节其贡献。校正后的未来特征通过候选特定的交叉注意力对轨迹进行评分，从而连接世界建模与规划选择。推理时仅使用过去与当前观测、自车状态及候选轨迹。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 全部 12,146 个场景中，以专家轨迹为条件评估：未来特征 MSE 相比 Normal 降低 5.35%，83.54% 的场景有改进；使用官方 EPDMS 评估器达到 91.05（百分制）。

Conclusion: 将校正后的未来表示整合进规划，可改善未来特征预测并获得有竞争力的轨迹选择效果。

Abstract: World models support autonomous driving by predicting the scene evolution associated with candidate trajectories. Driving dynamics differ in predictability, motivating a distinction between regular evolution and event-induced deviations that call for selective correction. We propose EditWM, a world model that decomposes future prediction into normal evolution and event-driven incremental correction in compact visual feature space. A trajectory-conditioned normal predictor provides the base forecast and is then frozen for correction learning. A correction decoder compares this forecast with observation history and planned actions, producing a bounded feature update whose contribution is regulated by a learned gate. The corrected future features condition trajectory scoring through candidate-specific cross-attention, linking world modeling to plan selection. At inference, EditWM uses only past and current observations, ego state, and candidate trajectories. Across all 12,146 NAVSIM navtest scenes, expert-trajectory-conditioned evaluation shows a 5.35\% reduction in future-feature MSE over Normal, with improvements in 83.54\% of scenes. The system achieves 91.05 EPDMS on a 100-point scale using the official EPDMS evaluator. These results demonstrate improved future-feature prediction and competitive trajectory selection when corrected future representations are integrated into planning.

</details>


### [154] [Embedding Physics Priors in Robot Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22319)
*Mattia Piccinini,Lucas Schulze,Alice Plebe,Matteo Saveriano,Thomas Beckers,Yuan Gao,Oleg Arenz,Baha Zarrouki,Dingrui Wang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Jan Peters,Johannes Betz,Gastone Pietro Rosati Papini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了"物理嵌入式机器人学习"领域，提出统一分类体系（物理引导的输入/数据/表示、物理编码的模型架构、物理约束的损失函数），并综述了动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与估计等应用及开源生态，指出物理先验是机器人特有的归纳偏置，可补充而非替代数据驱动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动方法在计算机视觉和自然语言处理中取得了显著成功，但机器人领域受限于数据稀缺、真实世界交互复杂以及可靠运行的需求。物理嵌入式机器人学习通过将物理先验嵌入学习算法，有望以机器人特有的归纳偏置弥补数据不足，提升泛化性、可解释性和样本效率。然而现有文献在术语、方法和应用领域上高度碎片化，难以评估这一不断增长的研究。

Method: 采用综述（survey）方法，涵盖广泛的物理先验、机器人应用和机器学习模型（从单层感知机到生成式基础模型）。提出统一分类体系，按物理嵌入方式将现有方法分为三类：物理引导的输入、数据与表示；物理编码的模型架构；物理约束的训练损失函数。基于该体系综述机器人动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与估计方法及相应开源软件生态。

Result: 建立了一个统一的分类框架以整合碎片化的文献，系统归纳了三类物理嵌入方式及其在主要机器人任务中的应用，梳理了相关开源软件生态，并识别出该领域的关键开放挑战与有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 物理先验提供了特别相关的机器人特有归纳偏置，它补充而非替代数据驱动学习，为实现更具泛化性、数据高效性和可信赖性的机器人系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: The rapid progress of artificial intelligence is reshaping robotics and accelerating the adoption of learning-based approaches. While purely data-driven methods have achieved remarkable success in computer vision and natural language processing, robotics remains constrained by limited data, complex real-world interactions, and the need for reliable operation. These challenges have motivated the exploration of physics-embedded robot learning, which embeds physics priors into learning algorithms. By encoding the underlying physical laws and constraints, physics priors can complement limited data with robotics-specific inductive biases, potentially improving generalization, interpretability, and sample efficiency. However, the literature on physics-embedded robot learning remains fragmented across terminology, methodologies, and application domains, making it difficult to assess this growing body of work. This survey reviews physics-embedded robot learning across a broad range of physics priors, robotics applications, and machine learning models, from single-layer perceptrons to generative foundation models. We adopt a unified taxonomy that classifies existing approaches according to their physics embedding: physics-guided inputs, data, and representations; physics-encoded model architectures; and physics-informed training loss functions. Building on this taxonomy, we review methods for robot dynamics learning, trajectory planning, prediction, control, and estimation, together with the corresponding open-source software ecosystem. We identify key open challenges, and outline promising future research directions. Overall, we argue that physics priors provide a particularly relevant robotics-specific inductive bias, complementing rather than replacing data-driven learning, and paving the way toward more generalizable, data-efficient, and trustworthy robotic systems.

</details>


### [155] [ReliCAD: From Uncertain LLM Generation to Reliable Parametric CAD Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22325)
*Peng Zheng,Xintong Dong,Chuanyang Li,Jiaxin Jing,Chuqi Han,Hailong Shen,Yanzhi Song,Zhouwang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReliCAD框架，将LLM的不确定生成转化为可靠的参数化CAD建模，实现99.8%有效率和0.8753 IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言驱动的参数化CAD建模中潜力巨大，但其概率性生成与CAD建模的确定性需求之间存在根本矛盾，导致可靠性、设计意图保持和几何有效性方面存在局限。现有方法依赖大规模标注数据、缺乏对设计意图的显式建模，且未充分利用CAD内核的确定性能力。

Method: ReliCAD通过显式设计意图建模，将用户需求转化为结构化设计规范，显式建模几何关系、拓扑依赖和特征构建顺序；生成约束感知的参数化指令，并通过Agent-ready API调用CAD内核执行几何构建与约束求解；记录运行时证据，采用验证-反馈机制评估生成模型与设计规范的一致性，实现错误定位与迭代修复。

Result: 在公开HistCAD生成数据集和自建多粒度CAD编辑数据集上，ReliCAD显著优于基线方法，达到99.8%的有效率和0.8753的IoU。

Conclusion: ReliCAD为自然语言交互式CAD建模提供了一种可验证、可修复且具有泛化性的方法。

Abstract: Large language models have shown considerable potential for natural-language-driven parametric CAD modeling. However, a fundamental contradiction exists between their probabilistic generation and the deterministic requirements of CAD modeling, resulting in limitations in reliability, design-intent preservation, and geometric validity. Existing methods typically rely on large-scale annotated datasets, lack explicit modeling of design intent, and underutilize the deterministic capabilities of CAD kernels. To address these limitations, we propose ReliCAD, a unified framework that transforms uncertain LLM generation into reliable parametric CAD modeling. Through explicit design-intent modeling, ReliCAD converts user requirements into structured design specifications and explicitly models geometric relations, topological dependencies, and feature construction order. It then generates constraint-aware parametric instructions and invokes the CAD kernel through an Agent-ready API to perform geometric construction and constraint solving. ReliCAD further records runtime evidence and employs a verification-feedback mechanism to assess consistency between the generated model and the design specifications, enabling error localization and iterative repair. Experiments on the public HistCAD generation dataset and our multi-granularity CAD editing dataset demonstrate that ReliCAD significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving 99.8\% validity rate and 0.8753 IoU. ReliCAD provides a verifiable, repairable, and generalizable approach to natural-language-interactive CAD modeling.

</details>


### [156] [AffordanceWAM: Affordance-Aware Joint World-Action Modeling for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22332)
*Jiadi You,Qize Yu,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhide Zhong,Yuran Wang,Bowen Ping,Jiaqi Liang,Zhenhao Shen,Haodong Yan,Yinchuan Li,Ruihai Wu,Xiaojuan Qi,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AffordanceWam，一种以 affordance（可供性）为共享接口的生成式世界-动作模型，用人类视频与机器人视频联合训练，实现可泛化的机器人操作与人类到机器人的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作需要预测场景演化、判断可交互位置并决定动作。带动作标注的机器人视频可直接监督控制，但成本高、多样性差；第一人称人类视频交互多样，却缺少机器人动作，且存在具身与外观差异。因此需要一种能跨人类/机器人数据共享的中间表示，以便在无人类动作标签、无需动作重定向的情况下实现迁移。

Method: AffordanceWam 用对象中心的时空 affordance 表示（Scalar Affordance 与 Affordance Heatmap），嵌入生成式未来世界。基于预训练视频扩散 Transformer，采用分别参数化的 World Expert 与 Action Expert，通过 Masked Joint Self-Attention 耦合，在统一的 flow-matching 目标下联合预测未来 RGB 观测、Scalar Affordance 场、Affordance Heatmap 与连续机器人动作。人类视频监督三个未来世界流，机器人轨迹额外提供动作监督。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、CALVIN ABC→D 及真实世界操作任务上，方法一致优于仅 RGB 与仅机器人数据的基线；在固定机器人监督下，RoboCasa 性能随带 affordance 标注的人类视频规模增长而单调提升。

Conclusion: affordance 可作为视觉-语言-动作学习与人类到机器人迁移的有效接口。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires predicting how a scene will evolve, identifying where interactions are feasible, and determining how to act. Action-labeled robot videos directly supervise control but are costly and limited in diversity, whereas egocentric human videos capture diverse interactions but lack robot actions and differ in embodiment and appearance. We introduce AffordanceWAM, an affordance-aware generative World Action Model that represents object-centric spatiotemporal affordance through Scalar Affordance and Affordance Heatmap, within the generated future World. This representation grounds visual prediction in task-relevant objects and interaction regions for action generation, and provides shared interaction targets across human and robot videos. Built on a pretrained video diffusion Transformer, AffordanceWAM uses separately parameterized World and Action Experts, coupled through Masked Joint Self-Attention, to jointly predict future RGB observations, Scalar Affordance fields, Affordance Heatmaps, and continuous robot actions under a unified flow-matching objective. Human videos supervise all three future-World streams, whereas robot trajectories additionally provide action supervision, enabling transfer without human action labels or retargeting. Experiments on RoboCasa, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over RGB-only and robot-data-only baselines. Under fixed robot supervision, RoboCasa performance improves monotonically as affordance-annotated human video scales. These results support affordance as an effective interface for both vision-language-action learning and human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [157] [Active Spatial Inspection for Effective and Efficient Embodied Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22385)
*Wenbin Wang,Xiang Bai,Yizhao Wang,Hang Sun,Dong Ren,Jie Qin,Qingquan Li,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间检查引导的主动探索框架ACE，通过证据落地的感知与曝光告知的移动建立空间明确的决策范式，显著提升具身探索的任务成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身探索框架依赖空间上粗糙且间接的暗示性线索与方向评估，难以判断线索是否充分及是否需要进一步检查，导致过早终止或过度延续，从而降低任务成功率与效率。

Method: ACE框架从空间明确的角度重新思考具身探索，将证据落地的感知与曝光告知的移动相耦合。证据落地感知通过细粒度定位与聚焦验证增强推理有效性，将暗示性线索转化为精确的决定性视觉支撑；曝光告知移动通过前瞻性优先化与回溯性抑制促进方向判断，选择有前景的方向实现高效空间推进。

Result: 大量实验表明，ACE在导航任务成功率上比先前最先进基线高18.0%，在问答探索效率上高10.3%。

Conclusion: ACE缓解了防止过早终止与避免不必要延续之间的张力，推动了有效且高效的具身探索。

Abstract: Achieving high task success and efficiency remains a central pursuit in embodied exploration. Existing frameworks typically guide agent behavior through a spatially coarse and indirect assessment of suggestive cues and directions, yet such designs may struggle to judge cue sufficiency and the need for further inspection, leading to early termination or excessive continuation and ultimately reducing task success and efficiency. This work rethinks embodied exploration from a spatially explicit standpoint and introduces the spatial-inspection-guided ACtive Exploration (ACE) framework coupling evidence-grounded perception with exposure-informed movement and establishing a spatially resolved decision paradigm. Evidence-grounded perception strengthens inferential validity by integrating granular localization with focused verification, turning suggestive cues into precise decisive visual support. Exposure-informed movement promotes directional judgment through prospective prioritization and retrospective suppression, selecting promising directions for efficient spatial progress. ACE alleviates the tension between preventing early termination and avoiding unnecessary continuation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE achieves 18.0% higher navigation task success and 10.3% higher exploration efficiency for question answering than prior state-of-the-art baselines, advancing effective and efficient embodied exploration.

</details>


### [158] [Behavior Trees for Robotic Systems: An Empirical Study on Practices and Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22404)
*Razan Ghzouli,Jennifer Horkoff,Daniel Strüber,Rebekka Wohlrab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究（汽车公司技术行动研究+34名机器人从业者调查），揭示了行为树（BT）在真实场景中的应用现状、实践者经验与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年行为树已成为协调机器人系统任务的主流行为模型，但关于其在真实场景中采用的实证证据有限，尤其缺乏来自学术与工业界实践者的经验支撑。缺乏实证可能导致研究社区提出的指南和工具虽理论上合理却与实际挑战不完全契合，进而造成临时性实践，阻碍软件复用、维护与演进。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，将在一家汽车公司开展的技术行动研究调查与对34名机器人从业者的问卷调查相结合。

Result: 行为树改善了团队沟通与机器人决策逻辑的可理解性，体现出超越任务协调的实用价值；但从业者在设计与集成方面面临诸多非平凡决策，因缺乏充分指南与工具支持而更加复杂，涉及架构与语言选择及与ROS集成（报告了观察到的模式）；还面临多因素粒度决策、对现有库的混合体验、最常用库的实现挑战与文档缺口、规划算法中节点排序决策困难，可扩展性看法尚无定论。

Conclusion: 当前库与实践的局限阻碍了更广泛采用；作者提出跨领域观察，并对采用行为树的从业者及致力于推进行为树采用实证理解的研究者给出启示。

Abstract: Over the last decade, behavior trees (BT) have become one of the dominant behavior models for coordinating missions of robotic systems. Yet empirical evidence on BT adoption in real-world contexts remains limited, especially regarding practitioners experiences and practices. Without practitioner-grounded evidence from both academic and industrial settings, the research community risks developing guidelines and tools that are plausible in principle but only partly aligned with real challenges. This scarcity of evidence leads to ad-hoc practices, impeding software reuse, maintenance, and evolution. This paper reports a mixed-methods study combining a technical action research investigation at an automotive company with a survey of 34 robotics practitioners. Our results indicate that BTs improve team communication and the understandability of robotic decision-making logic, reflecting BTs practical value beyond mission coordination. At the same time, practitioners face multiple non-trivial design and integration decisions, complicated by the lack of adequate guidelines and tool support. These decisions span architectural and language choices when implementing BTs and integrating them within ROS, for which we report observed patterns. Additionally, practitioners faced multi-factor granularity decisions and mixed experiences with current libraries. For the most used BT libraries, they reported implementation challenges and documentation gaps. Regarding planning algorithms, practitioners find deciding optimal BT node ordering challenging. Views on scalability remained inconclusive, and limitations in current libraries and practices hinder broader adoption. We conclude with cross-cutting observations and implications for both practitioners adopting BTs in robotic systems and researchers aiming to advance empirical understanding of BT adoption.

</details>


### [159] [VLPSA: Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions for Full-Body Safety of Learned Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22462)
*Meg Wilkinson,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLPSA框架，为VLA策略提供全身碰撞避免安全过滤，无需重训练，在SafeLIBERO和真实FR3上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽提升机器人操作的通用性，但学到的策略无法保证碰撞避免安全性，尤其在训练分布外环境中存在风险。

Method: 在线从感知数据合成Poisson安全函数(PSF)，构建控制屏障函数(CBF)，通过CBF-QP安全过滤器对全身及抓取物体(视为末端连杆延伸)施加约束；用布尔CBF组合融合PSF的双分辨率，兼顾实时性与关键区域空间精度。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO上，碰撞避免率从基线π0.5策略的23.1%提升至91.2%，且任务成功率更高，优于安全过滤基线；在杂乱动态场景与人为干扰下于Franka FR3上实现实时全身安全。

Conclusion: VLPSA无需重训练即可为VLA策略提供全身实时碰撞避免安全保障，兼具高避障率与任务成功率，适用于动态杂乱环境。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable increasingly general-purpose robotic manipulation, but such learned policies do not provide safety guarantees for collision avoidance---especially in environments outside of training distributions. This work presents Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions (VLPSA), a safety filtering framework that provides full-body safety for VLA policies in cluttered and dynamic environments without retraining. VLPSA synthesizes Poisson Safety Functions (PSF) online from perception data, yielding a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that is enforced through a CBF-QP safety filter over the full body and any grasped object, treated as an extension of the final robot link. To enable real-time deployment while maintaining fine spatial resolution in critical task regions, VLPSA combines dual resolutions of this PSF using Boolean CBF compositions. We evaluate VLPSA on SafeLIBERO against safety-filtering baselines, where it achieves the highest collision avoidance rate among the evaluated methods, increasing collision avoidance from 23.1% for the base $π_{0.5}$ policy to 91.2% while surpassing its task success rate. We further deploy VLPSA on a Franka FR3 in cluttered scenes with dynamic obstacles and human interference, demonstrating real-time full-body safety during manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [160] [Tracker-Free Robotic Ultrasound Calibration with a Spherical-Marker Phantom and Threshold-Free Center Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22483)
*Kyoungmo Koo,Guangshen Ma,Xueding Wang,Mark Draelos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外部跟踪器的机器人超声标定框架，使用球形标记体模和高度自动化的感知流程定位球心，单标记标定精度与三标记相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声标定对准确关联超声图像与机器人坐标系至关重要，但现有方法通常需要复杂体模或外部三维跟踪器，准确且自动化的标定仍具挑战。

Method: 开发无跟踪器机器人超声标定框架：采用球形标记体模与高度自动化的球心定位感知流程。提出无阈值图像处理方法，基于每幅超声图像的强度分布定位球形特征，消除手工调参的强度阈值；利用球状基准的多姿态观测估计超声到末端执行器的变换，无需外部跟踪或机器人基座坐标系中球心的先验定位。

Result: 12折交叉验证中，单标记标定的球心精度与精确度为1.75±0.51 mm和0.85±0.11 mm，三标记为1.72±0.51 mm和0.84±0.12 mm。三组重建中，单标记标定配准后点到面MAE±SD为圆锥0.41±0.35 mm、三棱柱0.50±0.41 mm，接近三标记的0.40±0.35 mm和0.48±0.40 mm。

Conclusion: 该无跟踪器、无阈值的标定框架仅用单球形标记即可达到与三标记标定相当的精度与稳健性，简化了标定流程并提升了跨成像设置的适应性。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound (US) calibration is essential for accurately relating US images to the robot coordinate system, but accurate and automated calibration remains challenging because existing methods often require complex phantoms or external 3D trackers. In this work, we develop a tracker-free robotic US calibration framework using a spherical-marker phantom and a highly automated perception pipeline for sphere-center localization. The proposed threshold-free image-processing method localizes the spherical feature based on each US image's intensity distribution, eliminating hand-tuned intensity thresholds and improving robustness across imaging settings without per-system retuning. Multi-pose observations of the spherical fiducial are then used to estimate the US-to-EE transformation without external tracking or prior localization of the sphere center in the robot base frame. We validate the proposed framework on a robotic US platform through repeated sphere scans and further assess the calibrated system using geometrically distinct phantoms with known CAD models. Across 12 cross-validation folds, single-marker calibration achieved sphere-center accuracy and precision of $1.75\pm0.51$ mm and $0.85\pm0.11$ mm, respectively, compared with $1.72\pm0.51$ mm and $0.84\pm0.12$ mm for three-marker calibration. Across the three reconstruction sets, the single-marker calibration yielded pooled post-registration point-to-surface MAE$\pm$SD values of $0.41\pm0.35$ mm for the cone and $0.50\pm0.41$ mm for the triangular prism, closely matching the three-marker results of $0.40\pm0.35$ mm and $0.48\pm0.40$ mm, respectively.

</details>


### [161] [Layered e-skin for Shear Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22493)
*Qingzheng Cong,Alexis W. M. Devillard,Abu Bakar Dawood,Xinxin Zhang,Wen Fan,Neri Niccolò Dei,Cem Suulker,Kaspar Althoefer,Etienne Burdet,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双层堆叠FSR阵列指尖传感器，结合弹性点阵隔层将剪切力转为层间位移，实现三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FSR阵列仅能进行法向力感知，缺乏剪切力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移检测能力，限制了机器人指尖的灵巧操作。

Method: 设计双层FSR阵列，中间加入柔性点阵弹性体隔层，使剪切载荷转化为可测的层间位移，产生层间压力中心（CoP）相对偏移；用基于物理的力矩平衡将层间CoP位移与剪切力关联，并用端到端CNN-GRU模型捕捉载荷相关压缩与接触重分布的非线性效应，以双层信号为输入。

Result: 模型对F_x的R²=0.914、F_y的R²=0.944，持续优于单层基线；机械臂操作实验中，深层跟踪手臂施加的平移与旋转，浅层跟踪接触面滑移；两层总压力响应之差的瞬态变化在滑移事件检测中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 双层FSR阵列可超越传统法向力感知，提供三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移事件检测能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a stacked two-layer force-sensing resistor (FSR) array designed for robotic fingertips that combines high-resolution pressure mapping with shear-force estimation. A compliant lattice elastomer spacer converts shear loading into a measurable inter-layer displacement, producing relative center-of-pressure (CoP) shifts between layers. A physics-based moment balance links inter-layer CoP displacement to shear force, while an end-to-end CNN--GRU model captures nonlinear effects from load-dependent compression and contact redistribution. This model, with both layers as input, achieves coefficients of determination $R^2 = 0.914$ for $F_x$ and $R^2 = 0.944$ for $F_y$, consistently outperforming single-layer baselines for shear-force estimation. Robotic manipulation experiments show that, for contact-motion tracking, the deep layer tracks the translation and rotation imposed by the robot arm, whereas the superficial layer tracks the slip at the contact surface. Transient changes in the difference between the total pressure responses of the two layers provide the best slip-event detection performance among the tested cues. These results demonstrate that two-layer FSR arrays can provide three-axis force estimation, contact-motion tracking, and slip-event detection beyond conventional normal-force sensing.

</details>


### [162] [Latent Policy Steering: An Efficient and Flexible Framework for Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22521)
*Yiqi Wang,Mrinal Verghese,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Policy Steering (LPS)框架，通过在所有具身之间共享的视觉动力学上进行预训练的世界模型，在测试时对已有策略进行引导搜索，实现高效的跨具身迁移，仅需50个目标具身演示即可显著提升Diffusion Policy和Pi0.5的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉运动策略的性能高度依赖训练数据的规模与质量，但真实世界的高质量演示采集成本高昂。已有的大规模机器人与人类数据集虽日益丰富，却因具身差异（embodiment gap）和动作空间不匹配而难以直接利用。因此，跨具身迁移——复用其他具身的经验来提升目标任务的学习——对于突破"每机器人单独采集数据"的瓶颈至关重要。

Method: LPS采用"具身无关预训练 + 目标具身微调 + 测试时潜空间搜索"的三段式流程：(1) 在多样具身上，用光流训练一个基于图像的世界模型（WM），捕捉跨具身共享的视觉动力学；(2) 用目标具身的机器人动作对WM进行微调；(3) 在WM的潜空间中搜索那些与微调数据保持接近的规划方案，从而将基础策略引导向更优动作。该框架与策略无关，可灵活适配不同策略而无需重新训练它们。

Result: 在Robomimic仿真和真实世界评测中，面对未见过的目标具身且仅有50个演示，LPS使Diffusion Policy的平均性能分别相对提升约16%（仿真）和62%（真实世界），使Pi0.5分别相对提升约8%和14%。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦具身间共享的视觉动力学并有效利用稀缺的目标具身数据，LPS以策略无关的方式实现了高效的跨具身迁移，为在单机器人数据采集之外扩展机器人学习提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: The performance of learned robot visuomotor policies depends heavily on the size and quality of their training data, yet collecting high-quality demonstrations remains costly for robots in the real world. Although large-scale robot and human datasets are increasingly available, embodiment gaps and mismatched action spaces make them difficult to leverage directly. Cross-embodiment transfer, reusing experience from other embodiments to improve learning on a target embodiment, is therefore crucial for scaling robot learning beyond per-robot data collection.
  In this work, we find that efficient transfer can be achieved by learning from what is shared across embodiments, the visual dynamics of how the world responds to motion, and by effectively exploiting the scarce target-embodiment data at test time. The proposed framework, called Latent Policy Steering (LPS), implements an embodiment-agnostic pretraining phase, which trains an image-based World Model (WM) with optical flow across diverse embodiments. The resulting WM is finetuned on the target embodiment with robot actions. It then steers the base policy toward better actions by searching in the WM's latent space for plans that stay close to the finetuning data. LPS is a policy-agnostic framework: it can flexibly accommodate different policies without having to retrain them. In Robomimic and real-world evaluations, LPS improves the average performance of Diffusion Policy relatively by 16% and 62%, and Pi0.5 by 8% and 14%, with only 50 demonstrations on an unseen target embodiment.

</details>


### [163] [FRAMES: Failure Recovery And Monitoring of Embodied Skills for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22538)
*Ajay Vikram Periasami,Xinyuan Luo,Haoyu Li,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRAMES 是一个面向 Unitree G1 人形机器人的失败感知监督框架，通过规划、监控、恢复与记忆模块提升长时序 loco-manipulation 任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 规划器虽能分解指令并选择可复用技能，但技能选择正确并不保证物理执行成功；在人形 loco-manipulation 中，接近、抓取、搬运或放置阶段的错误会使长时序计划失效。

Method: FRAMES 运行于 CEER 全身控制器之上：Planner Agent 通过参数化中层技能选择子任务；基于 VLM 的 Monitor Agent 利用时序多视角观测及结构化机器人与接触证据评估每个技能；检测到失败后停止当前技能并向 Recovery Agent 提供有依据的反馈；框架还包含复用技能经验的 Memory Module，以及基于深度与分割的几何 grounding。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 中独立评估监控模块，共 100 次试验（50 次失败、50 次成功，覆盖 5 个任务）：检测出 50 次失败中的 48 次，正确接受 50 次成功中的 46 次，总体准确率 94.0%。

Conclusion: 结果为监控组件提供了初步证据，完整恢复闭环的端到端评估仍在进行中。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) planners can decompose natural-language instructions and select reusable robot skills, but choosing the correct skill does not guarantee successful physical execution. This gap is especially important in humanoid loco-manipulation, where errors during approach, grasping, transport, or placement can invalidate the remainder of a long-horizon plan. We present FRAMES, a failure-aware supervisory framework for the Unitree G1 humanoid that operates above the CEER whole-body controller. A Planner Agent selects subtasks through parameterized mid-level skills, while a vision-language-model-based Monitor Agent evaluates each skill using temporal multi-view observations and structured robot and contact evidence. Detected failures stop the active skill and provide grounded feedback to a Recovery Agent. The framework further includes a Memory Module for reusing prior skill experience, and geometric grounding via depth and segmentation. We independently evaluate the monitoring module of the framework in MuJoCo using 100 trials comprising 50 failed and 50 successful executions across five tasks. The monitor detects 48 of 50 failures, correctly accepts 46 of 50 successful executions, and achieves 94.0% overall accuracy. These results provide initial evidence for the monitoring component, while end-to-end evaluation of the complete recovery loop remains ongoing.

</details>


### [164] [React When You Need To: Event-Triggered Asynchronous Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22587)
*Yansong Wu,Huaqing Li,Tianding Hou,Lingyun Chen,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略，根据场景变化自适应调整推理间隔，兼顾运动一致性与反应及时性，平均成功率95%，超出最强基线55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常预测连续动作块，导致执行过程中难以对环境变化做出反应；已有的异步推理方法虽提升了反应性，但依赖固定的推理间隔，无法自适应场景变化。

Method: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略，根据自上次推理以来观察到的场景变化动态调整推理间隔，从而在保持运动一致性的同时实现及时反应。

Result: 在静态与动态真实场景中均表现最佳，平均成功率达95%，比最强基线高出55个百分点。

Conclusion: 事件引导的动态推理能有效兼顾运动一致性与反应性，显著优于固定间隔的异步推理方案；代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly predict action chunks, limiting their ability to react to environmental changes during execution. Existing asynchronous inference methods improve reactivity but typically rely on a fixed inference gap. In this paper, we propose an event-guided dynamic inference strategy that adapts the inference gap according to scene changes observed since the previous inference. Thereby, it simultaneously preserves motion consistency and prompt reactivity. Across static and dynamic real-world settings, our method consistently performs best, averaging 95% success and exceeding the strongest baseline by 55 percentage points. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance. The project page is available at https://react-when-you-need-to.github.io/.

</details>


### [165] [REBOOT: From Failure to Recovery - A Dataset and Benchmark for Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22591)
*Nana Yaw Owusu Ofori-Ampofo,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Joseph Thekinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REBOOT是首个以"失败"为核心信号的机器人操作基准，含2160条示范、18个精密装配任务、5个共享阶段，并提供专家恢复轨迹与阶段级失败标注，用于超越二元成功率的细粒度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习策略存在典型失败模式（不确定状态停滞、接触对齐漂移、精密任务毫米级偏差），但训练数据多为成功示范，基准评估又常简化为二元成功率，导致恢复监督缺失、失败位置难以分析。

Method: 构建REBOOT基准：18个精密装配任务拆解为Align(pick)、Engage(pick)、Transport、Align(place)、Engage(place)五个共享阶段；在各阶段注入失败并配对专家恢复轨迹；标注旋转对称性、接合间隙精度层级、装配方向（install-remove配对）；按阶段与失败类别标注；提供四视角同步RGB-D与阶段级成功/失败条件自然语言描述；一半为专家示范，一半为模仿学习策略rollout中采样得到的恢复示范。

Result: 对action-chunked transformer、diffusion与π0-FAST策略进行基准测试，采用阶段级完成率评估，揭示了二元评估所掩盖的模型特异性失败点。

Conclusion: 将失败作为一等信号可提供恢复监督并支持阶段级归因分析，REBOOT为机器人操作的细粒度失败诊断与恢复学习提供了数据与评估基础。

Abstract: Robot learning policies fail in characteristic ways: they stall in uncertain states, drift during contact-rich alignment, and miss targets by millimetres in precision tasks. Yet training datasets consist largely of successful demonstrations, while real-world benchmarks often reduce performance to binary success. This limits both supervision for recovery and analysis of where failures occur. We introduce REBOOT (Recovery Episode Benchmark for Off-nominal Trajectories), the first robot manipulation benchmark designed around failure as a first-class signal. REBOOT contains 2,160 demonstrations across 18 precision assembly tasks, each decomposed into five shared phases: Align(pick), Engage(pick), Transport, Align(place), and Engage(place), enabling phase-level evaluation beyond terminal success. Failures are introduced across phases and paired with expert recovery trajectories that return the system to a valid continuation state. Tasks are annotated with rotational symmetry, engagement-clearance precision tier, and assembly direction through matched install-remove pairs. Failure episodes are labeled by phase and categorical failure mode, enabling attribution to kinematic stage and tolerance violation. Data includes synchronized RGB-D observations from four viewpoints and grounded natural-language descriptions of phase-level success and failure conditions. Half the dataset contains expert demonstrations; the other half contains recovery demonstrations sampled to reflect failures observed in imitation-learned policy rollouts. We benchmark action-chunked transformer, diffusion, and $π_0$-FAST policies using phase-level completion rates, revealing model-specific failure points hidden by binary evaluation. Dataset and code: https://nanayawoa.github.io/REBOOT

</details>


### [166] [Characterizing Wildlife Response to Biomimetic and Conventional Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22594)
*Huy Pham,Levi Cai,Yogesh Girdhar,Daniela Rus,Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次构建数据集，比较鱼类对传统推进器AUV、仿生海龟鳍驱动AUV与潜水员的扰动反应，发现两种AUV均可引起鱼类行为变化，仿生AUV点估计总体更优，但差异经多重比较校正后不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV是采集海洋生态数据的可扩展替代方案，但其可能惊扰当地动物，导致行为差异，削弱其作为科学工具的价值。作者希望通过仿生设计（模仿非捕食性动物的形态与行为）来减少这种不利反应，因此需要量化比较不同AUV与潜水员对鱼类的扰动。

Method: 在加勒比海珊瑚礁生物多样性热点区域开展实验，对比三种情形下的鱼类扰动：传统推进器驱动AUV、受海龟启发的鳍驱动仿生AUV、以及潜水员。利用计算机视觉对场景图像进行定位与分析，研究鱼类行为变化。

Result: 两种AUV都会引起可测量的鱼类行为变化。经多重比较校正后，两种机器人之间的差异均不显著，但点估计总体倾向于仿生AUV，这支持开展更大规模研究以分辨微小效应并进一步优化设计。同时观察到潜水员样带中的反应大于AUV样带，但该探索性比较样本量小且存在若干混杂因素。

Conclusion: 受操作与实验限制，结论属于初步性质。本研究为社区提供了首个已知数据集与基准，用于量化仿生机器人野外生态监测中的行为影响。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are a promising alternative to divers for scalable collection of natural ocean ecology data. However, these robots may disturb local fauna and cause drastic behavioral differences compared to other monitoring techniques, decreasing their value as scientific tools. A promising prospect is to make AUVs that are more biomimetic, with the hope that taking on the form and behavior of a non-predatory animal may reduce adverse responses. We present the first dataset comparing fish disturbance in response to a conventional thruster-driven AUV, a sea turtle inspired flipper-driven AUV, and a diver. Experiments were conducted at a biodiversity hotspot in a Caribbean reef, and images of the scene were analyzed using computer vision to localize and study fish behavior change. Both AUVs cause measurable changes in fish behavior. Although no between-robot differences remain significant after correction for multiple comparisons, point estimates generally favor the biomimetic AUV, motivating larger studies capable of resolving modest effects and further design changes to optimize for disturbance. We also observe larger responses during diver transects than during AUV transects, although this exploratory comparison is based on a small diver sample with several confounds. While conclusions must be taken as preliminary due to operational and experimental limitations, this study provides the community with a first known dataset and benchmarks to quantify the behavioral impacts of biomimetic robots for ecological monitoring in the wild.

</details>


### [167] [VT-Bridge: Bridging Pretrained Foundation VLAs to VTLAs via Lightweight Residual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22606)
*Yansong Wu,Tuo Yang,Rongping Zhao,Lingyun Chen,Xiao Chen,Junnan Li,Fan Wu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-Bridge 是一种轻量级残差适配策略，将预训练 VLA 基础模型桥接为 VTLA 模型，大幅降低视觉-触觉数据与算力需求，在四类富接触操作任务上平均将完成率从 11.7% 提升至 62.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VTLA 模型在富接触操作中优于 VLA，但其开发受制于海量视觉-触觉数据和计算资源，门槛很高。作者希望找到一种低成本方案，无需从零训练或改动原有 VLA 架构即可获得触觉能力。

Method: 提出 VT-Bridge 残差适配策略：在多种 VLA 骨干上采用一致的轻量残差适配器架构，并配以骨干专属权重，在机器人执行频率下对动作进行精修。每个任务仅需最多 50 条视觉-触觉示范，用于微调 VLA 骨干并训练一个仅 0.98M 参数的残差适配器。

Result: 在 π0、π0.5 和 SmolVLA 三种代表性 VLA 骨干、四类富接触操作任务上验证一致有效；平均任务完成率由仅做任务级 VLA 微调的 11.7% 提升至 62.9%。

Conclusion: VT-Bridge 具有跨 VLA 架构的广泛适用性、有效性和易用性，为富接触操作提供了一条低数据、低算力的可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Tactile-Language-Action (VTLA) models have demonstrated clear advantages over Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in contact-rich manipulation. However, developing VTLA models is severely constrained by the massive amounts of vision-tactile data and computational resources required. To address this bottleneck, we propose VT-Bridge, a lightweight residual adaptation strategy that bridges pretrained foundation VLAs to VTLAs. Rather than training a VTLA model from scratch or modifying the original architecture of a pretrained VLA, VT-Bridge employs an identical lightweight residual-adapter architecture across VLA backbones and uses backbone-specific weights to refine actions at the robot execution frequency. This design substantially lowers the data and training barriers. Specifically, it requires up to 50 vision-tactile demonstrations per task to fine-tune a VLA backbone and train a 0.98M-parameter residual adapter. Experiments with three representative VLA backbones ($π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, and SmolVLA) across four contact-rich manipulation tasks further demonstrate its consistent effectiveness across VLA architectures. On average, VT-Bridge raises the task completion rate from 11.7% with task-level VLA fine-tuning alone to 62.9%. Together, these findings demonstrate the broad applicability, effectiveness, and accessibility of VT-Bridge for contact-rich manipulation. The project page is available at https://hoxnocha.github.io/vt-bridge-web/.

</details>


### [168] [Becoming a Fruit Ninja: Real-Time Probabilistic Kinodynamic Planning for Manipulator Projectile Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22608)
*Lucas Chen,Austin Garrett,Andrew Niu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRUITNINJA：一种基于GPU批量采样树的任意时间规划器，用于机械臂高速拦截抛掷物体，在真实标定仿真中切割成功率显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拦截抛掷物体是困难的动态操作问题：需在毫秒内让机械臂在物体路径的某一点到达并满足执行器限制；若还需"切割"，则接触时的速度与姿态也被约束。目标是求解机器人状态与到达时间的可行子集，需在其中快速找到满足执行器限制的解。

Method: 提出FRUITNINJA，一种在GPU上批量扩展搜索树的任意时间采样规划器，朝向"拦截流形"生长。每条边为精确三次曲线，其行程时间通过对机械臂动力学的并行搜索确定，从而保证每条边都满足执行器限制。规划依据一个风险感知目标函数排序，该函数考虑物体位置与机械臂到达时间的不确定性。

Result: 在Franka Research 3上，于标定的实时仿真中与六个基线对比：FRUITNINJA在无障碍场景切割成功率为96.7%，在五个障碍物场景为68.3%；最佳基线分别为68.3%和35.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合GPU批量采样、精确轨迹时间搜索与风险感知排序，可有效解决毫秒级动态拦截/切割问题，并在有无障碍场景下均大幅超越现有基线。

Abstract: Projectile interception is a challenging dynamic manipulation problem. Intercepting a thrown object with a robot arm requires reaching a point on the object's path as the object passes through it. Slicing also fixes the blade's velocity and orientation at contact. The goal is therefore a subset of the states of the robot and arrival times that moves as the object falls, and the arm must reach it within its actuator limits in milliseconds. We present FRUITNINJA, an anytime sampling-based planner that grows a tree on the GPU in batches toward the interception manifold. Each edge is an exact cubic whose travel time is found by a parallel search against the arm's dynamics, so every edge satisfies the actuator limits. Plans are ranked by a risk-aware objective over the uncertainty in the object's position and the arm's arrival time. We evaluate on a Franka Research 3 against six baselines in a calibrated real-time simulator, where FRUITNINJA cuts 96.7% of tosses in the open and 68.3% among five obstacles, versus the best baseline's 68.3% and 35.0% respectively.

</details>


### [169] [From Documented Strengths to Force Limits: Material-Informed Robotic Insertion for Construction Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22609)
*Lin He,Yanyi Chen,Haofei Sun,Lingyao Li,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE将文献中的材料强度证据转为机器人插入装配的轴向承载力估计并据此设定力限，显著降低承载力误差并实现可追溯的插入决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配插入中，材料与装配条件的变化使得难以选取既完成任务又不超装配承载力的接触力；建筑文档虽包含材料及其条件的工程知识，但难以将其转化为特定装配的载荷限值。

Method: SAGE将大语言模型限制为从检索到的段落与表格中抽取拉伸与压缩强度并记录来源；再用响应模型插值离线有限元解，将强度与装配条件换算为轴向承载力。正间隙配合时用该承载力设定策略的轴向力限；过盈配合时与实测支撑需求比较以决定是否准入。

Result: 主基准上平均承载力误差由直接LLM估计的80.65%降至10.74%；不重新拟合时在16个新几何上平均误差为8.00%。在指定支撑释放模型下，62次计分仿真中正确分类59次，且误差均为保守型。xArm6实机演示中以材料文档为输入，13次试验完成9次物理插入。

Conclusion: 将文档解读交给LLM、把力计算交给显式力学模型，可产生准确的承载力估计与可追溯的插入决策。

Abstract: Insertion is a fundamental operation in robotic construction assembly, where variations in material properties and assembly conditions make it difficult to select contact forces that complete the task without exceeding the assembly's capacity. Although construction documents encode engineering knowledge about materials and their conditions, translating this knowledge into load limits for a specific assembly remains difficult. This paper presents SAGE (Source-grounded Assembly Gating and Execution), a system that converts documented material evidence into capacity estimates for robotic insertion. SAGE restricts a large language model (LLM) to extracting tensile and compressive strengths from retrieved passages and tables and records their sources. A response model then interpolates offline finite element (FE) solutions to convert these strengths and the assembly conditions into axial load capacity. For fits with positive clearance, the estimated capacity sets the policy's axial force limit; for interference fits, it is compared with measured support demand to determine admission. On the primary benchmark, SAGE reduces mean capacity error from 80.65\% for direct LLM estimates based on the same evidence to 10.74\%. Without refitting, the mean error remains 8.00\% on 16 additional geometries. Under the assigned support release model, SAGE correctly classifies 59 of 62 scored simulation runs, with only conservative errors. In recorded xArm6 demonstrations, SAGE takes material documents as input and completes physical insertion in 9 of 13 trials. These results show that assigning document interpretation to the LLM and force calculation to an explicit mechanical model produces accurate capacity estimates and traceable insertion decisions.

</details>


### [170] [HIGenNTO: Scalable Humanoid Interaction Generation via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22611)
*Lalit Jayanti,Kashu Yamazaki,Yuto Shibata,Kotaro Amaya,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HIGenNTO框架，通过在预训练文本条件运动模型的初始噪声上做优化，在稀疏时空与场景约束下合成人形机器人交互运动参考，并可直接在真实机器人上执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人可从运动学人体参考中学习复杂技能，但接触丰富的交互难以获得可靠参考：动作捕捉在遮挡和紧密接触下退化，重定向又引入接触与几何不一致。因此需要一种无需动捕、能同时满足接触、避碰与支撑约束的运动参考生成方法。

Method: HIGenNTO将交互运动生成本身建模为对预训练文本条件运动模型"初始噪声"的优化问题，在稀疏的时空约束与场景约束下进行求解。同一套公式即可满足期望接触、避免碰撞并维持稳定支撑，同时保留先验模型的真实感与时间连贯性；既能从零生成交互运动，也能分阶段组合长时程行为。

Result: 在机器人-环境和机器人-物体任务上，生成的运动会由跟踪策略在仿真中执行，并用于训练仅依赖机载感知的深度条件视觉运动策略。策略部署在Unitree G1上完成四类接触丰富任务；此外任务规格可由编码智能体编写，自动编译为提示、约束与场景程序，贡献了八个评测任务中的三个及另外四种行为。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了从高层任务描述到物理可执行人形交互的可扩展路径，验证了噪声优化式生成参考在仿真与真实机器人上的有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can acquire complex skills by imitating kinematic humanoid motion references, yet reliable references for contact-rich interactions remain difficult to obtain: motion capture deteriorates under occlusion and close physical contact, while retargeting introduces additional contact and geometric inconsistencies. We present HIGenNTO, a framework that synthesizes humanoid-scene interaction motion references by optimizing the initial noise of a pretrained text-conditioned motion model under sparse spatiotemporal and scene constraints. The same formulation satisfies desired contacts, avoids collisions, and maintains stable support while retaining the prior's realism and temporal coherence, generating interaction motions from scratch and composing long-horizon behaviors stage-wise. Across robot-environment and robot-object tasks, HIGenNTO produces motions that can be executed by tracking policies in simulation and used to train depth-conditioned visuomotor policies operating solely from onboard sensing. We deploy these policies on a Unitree G1 across four contact-rich tasks. Finally, the task specifications themselves can be written by a coding agent, which proposes interaction tasks and compiles them into prompt, constraint, and scene programs, authoring three of our eight evaluated tasks and four further behaviors. Together, these results establish a scalable path from high-level task descriptions to physically executable humanoid interactions.

</details>


### [171] [Simultaneous Forward and Inverse Human-in-the-Loop Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22630)
*Kyeongwon Park,Steven H. Collins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SFIHILO方法，同时进行前向与逆向人在回路优化，高效推断个体偏好奖励并优化控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，用户重视的主观体验及偏好因人、因情境而异，但往往未知。辅助场景下执行控制策略、测量生物力学/生理结果并收集用户反馈的实验成本高，限制了查询与优化迭代次数。

Method: SFIHILO引导一个前向模型，从控制策略预测用户结果；用该模型进行主动查询以加速逆向奖励学习；随后无需额外用户试验即可优化最终控制策略。通过评估候选策略在前向与逆向信念上的期望不确定性降低来打分，并针对区域偏好边界以稳健地指导同时学习。

Result: 仿真表明，SFIHILO在用户异质性、结果维度、精度要求、噪声水平及非平稳性下均有效；与互信息方法相比，所提主动查询策略显著提升了逆向学习的样本效率，同时保持前向模型精度。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了推断潜在人类目标的能力，有助于实现更具可迁移性和有效性的人机交互。

Abstract: Subjective user experience is important to human-robot interaction, but the outcomes users value, and how those preferences vary across individuals and contexts, are often unknown.
